राकेश कुमार, सचिव 


ORDER 
New Delhi, the 22th October, 2026 


O.N. 122.—WHEREAS, the General Election to Legislative Assembly of Haryana, 2024 for 35-Safidon 
Assembly Constituency was held in pursuance of the Election Commission of India Notification No.464/ECI/HAR- 
LA/2024, dated 50 September, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per Section 78 of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, every contesting candidate at 
an election shall, within 30 days from the date of election of the returned candidate, lodge with the District Election 
Officer an account of his/her election expenses which shall be true copy of the account kept by him/her or his/her 
election agent under Section 77 of the said act; and 


[भाग 1---खण्ड 3(iii)] भारत का राजपत्र : अक्तूबर 31, 2026/ कार्तिक 9, 1948 313 


WHEREAS, the result of the above-mentioned election for 35-Safidon Assembly Constituency of Haryana, 
2024 was declared by the Returning Officer on 87 October, 2024 and hence the last date for lodging the account of 
Election Expenses was 77 November, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per the report submitted by DEO, Jind report dated 08.11.2024 forwarded by the Chief 
Electoral Officer, Haryana vide his letter no. 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 dated 05.02.2025, 
under sub-rule (1) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Sushil Bairagi a contesting candidate 
from 35-Safidon Assembly Constituency of state of Haryana has failed to lodge account of election expenses, as 
required under law; and 


WHEREAS, on the basis of the said report of the District Election Officer, Jind a Show-Cause notice No. 
76/HR-LA/Exp. Account/2024/CEMS-I, dated30™ April, 2025 was issued under sub rule (5) of Rule 89 of the 
Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961 by the Election Commission of India to Sh. Sushil Bairagi for not lodging of his 
account of Election Expenses; and 


WHEREAS, through the above said Show Cause Notice and under sub rule (6) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of 
Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Sushil Bairagi was directed to submit representation in writing to the Commission 
explaining the reasons for not lodging the account and also to lodge account of election expenses with the District 
Election Officer, Jind District within 20 days from the date of receipt of the notice; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind vide his letter no. Elec-2026/1387 dated 17.09.2026 has 
informed that the notice was duly served and received by Sh. Sushil Bairagi, on 09.05.2025; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind in his Supplementary Report, vide its letter no. Elec- 
2025/757 dated 10.06.2025 has reported that Sh. Sushil Bairagi has not submitted account of election expenses. 
Further, after receipt of the said notice, Sh. Sushil Bairagi has neither furnished any reason nor explanation to the 
Election Commission of India, for failure to lodge the account of his election expenses as prescribed under law; and 


WHEREAS, Section 10 A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951 provides that:- 
“If the Election Commission is satisfied that a person- 


(a) has failed to lodge an account of election expenses, within the time and in the manner required by or 
under this Act, and 


(b) has no good reason or justification for the failure, the Election Commission shall, by order published 
in the Official Gazette, declare him to be disqualified and any such person shall be disqualified for a 
period of three years from the date of the order.”’; 


WHEREAS, on the basis of facts and available records, the Commission is satisfied that Sh. Sushil Bairagi 
has failed to lodge account of his election expenses and has no good reason or justification for the failure to do so; and 


NOW, THEREFORE, in pursuance of Section 10A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, the 
Election Commission of India hereby declares Sh. Sushil Bairagi, VPO Bahadurgarh, Post Office Silla Kheri, 
Tehsil Safidon, District Jind, Haryana, a contesting candidate for 35-Safidon Assembly Constituency of 
Haryanain General Election to Legislative Assembly, 2024 to be disqualified for being chosen as and for being a 
member of either House of the Parliament or the Legislative Assembly or the Legislative Council of a State or Union 
Territory for a period of three years from the date of this order. 


[F. No. 76/HR-LA/35/2024/CEMS-I] 
RAKESH KUMAR, Secy.

नई दिल्‍ली, 22 अक्तूबर, 2026 


आ.अ. 123.— यतः, भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा दिनांक 5 सितम्बर, 2024 की अधिसूचना 
सं0 464/भा0नि0आ0/हरि0-वि0स0/2024 के अनुसरण में हरियाणा राज्य के 35-सफीदों विधान सभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र के 
लिए साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 आयोजित किया गया था; और 


यतः, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 78 के अनुसार, किसी निर्वाचन में, निर्वाचन लड़ने वाला 
प्रत्येक अभ्यर्थी, निर्वाचित अभ्यर्थी के निर्वाचन की तारीख से 30 दिनों के भीतर अपने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा जिला 
निर्वाचन अधिकारी के पास दाखिल करेगा जो उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 77 के अंतर्गत उसके द्वारा या उसके निर्वाचन 
अभिकर्ता (एजेंट)द्वारा रखे गए लेखे की एक सत्य प्रति होगी; और 


314 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : OCTOBER 31, 2026 / KARTIKA 9, 1948 [PART II—SEC. 3(iii)] 


यतः, हरियाणा राज्य के 35-सफीदों विधान सभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र के साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 का परिणाम 
रिटर्निंग आफिसर द्वारा दिनांक 8 अक्तूबर, 2024 को घोषित किया गया था और इस प्रकार निर्वाचन व्यय के लेखे दाखिल 
करने की अन्तिम तारीख 7 नवम्बर, 2024 थी; और 


यतः, निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (1) के अंतर्गत, जिला निर्वाचन 
अधिकारी, जीन्द की रिपोर्ट दिनांक 08.11.2024 जो मुख्य निर्वाचन अधिकारी, हरियाणा से दिनांक 05.02.2025 पत्र 
सं0 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 को प्राप्त हुई थी, के अनुसार श्री मोनू लोहान जो 
हरियाणा राज्य के 35-सफीदों विधानसभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र, 2024 से निर्वाचन लड़ने वाले अभ्यर्थी हैं, विधि के अंतर्गत 
यथापेक्षित रीति से अपने निर्वाचन व्ययों का लेखा दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द की उक्त रिपोर्ट के आधार पर भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा निर्वाचनों का 
संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (5) के अंतर्गत श्री मोनू लोहान को अपने निर्वाचन व्यय के लेखे प्रस्तुत 
नहीं करने के लिए कारण बताओ नोटिस सं. 76/हरि/वि.स./व्यय लेखा/2024/सी.ई.एम.एस.-1 दिनांकः 30 अप्रैल, 2025 
को जारी किया गया था, और 


यतः, उपर्युक्त कारण बताओ नोटिस के जरिए और निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप- 
नियम (6) के अनुसार श्री मोनू लोहान को लेखा प्रस्तुत न किए जाने संबंधी कारणों को स्पष्ट करते हुए, आयोग को अपना 
अभ्यावेदन लिखित रूप में प्रस्तुत करने और नोटिस के प्राप्त होने की तारीख से 20 दिनो के भीतर जिला निर्वाचन

सूचित किया है कि उक्त कारण बताओ नोटिस विधिवत रूप से श्री मोनू लोहान की भाभी (रैणु) के द्वारा दिनांक 
09.05.2025 को तामील किया गया था, और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द ने पत्र सं0- निर्वाचन-2025/757 दिनांक 10.06.2025 के द्वारा प्रस्तुत 
की गई अनुपूरक रिपोर्ट में यह बताया है कि श्री मोनू लोहान ने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा प्रस्तुत नहीं किया है। इसके 
अतिरिक्त, उक्त नोटिस की पावती के उपरांत, श्री मोनू लोहान ने विधि के अंतर्गत यथा विहित लेखा दाखिल करने में अपनी 
असफलता के लिए भारत निर्वाचन आयोग को न तो कोई कारण बताया है और न ही कोई स्पष्टीकरण दिया है, और 


यतः, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 10क में यह उपबंधित किया गया है किः 
“यदि Parry आयोग का समाधान at जाता है कि कोई व्यक्ति 


(4) निवतच्तिन व्ययों का लेखा उस समय के भीतर और रीति में जैसी इस अधिनियम के द्वारा या अधीन अपेक्षित 
है; दाखिल करने में असफल रहा है, और 


(खि) उस असफलता के लिए कोई अच्छा कारण अथवा Aaa नहीं रखता है; तो निर्वाचन आयोग; 
शायकीय राजपत्र में प्रकाशित आदेश द्वारा उसको निराहित घोषित करेगा और ऐसा व्यक्ति उस आदेश की 
तारीख से तीन वर्ष की कालावधि के लिए fated होगा।

निर्वाचन व्यय के लेखे दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं और उनके पास ऐसा करने में असफल रहने के लिए कोई भी उचित 
कारण अथवा औचित्य नहीं है, और 


अतः, अब, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 10क के अनुसरण में भारत निर्वाचन आयोग एतद्वारा 
घोषणा करता है कि विधान सभा के साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 में हरियाणा राज्य के 35-सफीदों विधान सभा निर्वाचन 
क्षेत्र से निर्वाचन लड़नेवाले अभ्यर्थी श्री मोनू लोहान, गांव गांगोली, तहसील सफीदों, जिला जीन्द, हरियाणा, इस आदेश 
की तारीख से तीन वर्ष की अवधि के लिए संसद के किसी भी सदन अथवा राज्य अथवा संघ राज्य क्षेत्र की विधानसभा 
अथवा विधान परिषद का सदस्य चुने जाने अथवा होने के लिए Pacts होंगे। 


[फा. सं. 76/हरि.-वि.स./35/2024/सी.ई.एम.एस.-1] 


राकेश कुमार, सचिव 


[भाग 1---खण्ड 3(iii)] भारत का राजपत्र : अक्तूबर 31, 2026/ कार्तिक 9, 1948 315 


ORDER 
New Delhi, the 22th October, 2026 


O.N. 123.— WHEREAS, the General Election to Legislative Assembly of Haryana, 2024 for 35-Safidon 
Assembly Constituency was held in pursuance of the Election Commission of India Notification No. 464/ECI/HAR- 
LA/2024, dated 50 September, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per Section 78 of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, every contesting candidate at 
an election shall, within 30 days from the date of election of the returned candidate, lodge with the District Election 
Officer an account of his/her election expenses which shall be true copy of the account kept by him/her or his/her 
election agent under Section 77 of the said act; and 


WHEREAS, the result of the above-mentioned election for 35-Safidon Assembly Constituency of Haryana, 
2024 was declared by the Returning Officer on 87 October, 2024 and hence the last date for lodging the account of 
Election Expenses was 77 November, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per the report submitted by DEO, Jind report dated 08.11.2024 forwarded by the Chief 
Electoral Officer, Haryana vide his letter no. 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 dated 05.02.2025, 
under sub-rule (1) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Monu Lohan a contesting candidate from 
35-Safidon Assembly Constituency of state of Haryana has failed to lodge account of election expenses, as required 
under law; and 


WHEREAS, on the basis of the said report of the District Election Officer, Jind a Show-Cause notice No. 
76/HR-LA/Exp. Account/2024/CEMS-I, dated 307 April, 2025 was issued under sub rule (5) of Rule 89 of the 
Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961 by the Election Commission of India to Sh. Monu Lohan for not lodging of his 
account of Election Expenses; and 


WHEREAS, through the above said Show Cause Notice and under sub rule (6) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of 
Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Monu Lohan was directed to submit representation in writing to the Commission 
explaining the reasons for not lodging the account and also to lodge account of election expenses with the District 
Election Officer, Jind District within 20 days from the date of receipt of the notice; and 


WHEREAS, theDistrict Election Officer, Jind vide his letter no. Elec-2026/1387 dated 17.09.2026 has 
informed that the notice was duly served and received by Sh. Monu Lohan's sister-in-law (name Smt. Renu), on 
09.05.2025; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind in his Supplementary Report, vide its letter no. Elec- 
2025/757 dated 10.06.2025 has reported that Sh. Monu Lohan has not submitted account of election expenses. 
Further, after receipt of the said notice, Sh. Monu Lohan has neither furnished any reason nor explanation to the 
Election Commission of India, for failure to lodge the account of his election expenses as prescribed under law; and 


WHEREAS, Section 10 A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951 provides that:- 
“If the Election Commission is satisfied that a person- 


(a) has failed to lodge an account of election expenses, within the time and in the manner required by or 
under this Act, and 


(b) has no good reason or justification for the failure, the Election Commission shall, by order published 
in the Official Gazette, declare him to be disqualified and any such person shall be disqualified for a 
period of three years from the date of the order.”’; 


WHEREAS, on the basis of facts and available records, the Commission is satisfied that Sh. Monu Lohan 
has failed to lodge account of his election expenses and has no good reason or justification for the failure to do so; and 


NOW, THEREFORE, in pursuance of Section 10A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, the 
Election Commission of India hereby declares Sh. Monu Lohan, Village Gangoli, Tehsil Safidon, District Jind, 
Haryana, a contesting candidate for 35-Safidon Assembly Constituency of Haryana in General Election to 
Legislative Assembly, 2024 to be disqualified for being chosen as and for being a member of either House of the 
Parliament or the Legislative Assembly or the Legislative Council of a State or Union Territory for a period of three 
years from the date of this order. 


[F. No. 76/HR-LA/35/2024/CEMS-I] 
RAKESH KUMAR, Secy.

आ.अ. 124.-- यतः, भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा दिनांक 5 सितम्बर, 2024 की अधिसूचना 
सं0 464/भा0नि0आ0/हरि0-वि0स0/2024 के अनुसरण में हरियाणा राज्य के 35-सफीदों विधान सभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र के 
लिए साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 आयोजित किया गया था; और 


यतः, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 78 के अनुसार, किसी निर्वाचन में, निर्वाचन लड़ने 
वाला प्रत्येक अभ्यर्थी, निर्वाचित अभ्यर्थी के निर्वाचन की तारीख से 30 दिनों के भीतर अपने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा जिला 
निर्वाचन अधिकारी के पास दाखिल करेगा जो उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 77 के अंतर्गत उसके द्वारा या उसके निर्वाचन 
अभिकर्ता (एजेंट)द्वारा रखे गए लेखे की एक सत्य प्रति होगी; और 


यतः, हरियाणा राज्य के 35-सफीदों विधान सभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र के साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 का 
परिणाम रिटर्निंग आफिसर द्वारा दिनांक 8 अक्तूबर, 2024 को घोषित किया गया था और इस प्रकार निर्वाचन व्यय के 
लेखे दाखिल करने की अन्तिम तारीख 7 नवम्बर, 2024 थी; और 


यतः, निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (1) के अंतर्गत, जिला निर्वाचन

ORDER 
New Delhi, the 22th October, 2026 


O.N. 124.—WHEREAS, the General Election to Legislative Assembly of Haryana, 2024 for 
35-Safidon Assembly Constituency was held in pursuance of the Election Commission of India Notification 
No.464/ECI/HAR-LA/2024, dated 5" September, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per Section 78 of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, every contesting candidate at 
an election shall, within 30 days from the date of election of the returned candidate, lodge with the District Election 
Officer an account of his/her election expenses which shall be true copy of the account kept by him/her or his/her 
election agent under Section 77 of the said act; and 


WHEREAS, the result of the above-mentioned election for 35-Safidon Assembly Constituency of Haryana, 
2024 was declared by the Returning Officer on 87 October, 2024 and hence the last date for lodging the account of 
Election Expenses was 77 November, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per the report submitted by DEO, Jind report dated 08.11.2024 forwarded by the Chief 
Electoral Officer, Haryana vide his letter no. 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 dated 05.02.2025, 
under sub-rule (1) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Sushil Kumar a contesting candidate from 
35-Safidon Assembly Constituency of state of Haryana has failed to lodge account of election expenses, as required 
under law; and 


WHEREAS, on the basis of the said report of the District Election Officer, Jind a Show-Cause notice No. 
76/HR-LA/Exp. Account/2024/CEMS-I, dated 30™ April, 2025 was issued under sub rule (5) of Rule 89 of the 
Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961 by the Election Commission of India to Sh. Sushil Kumar for not lodging of his 
account of Election Expenses; and 


WHEREAS, through the above said Show Cause Notice and under sub rule (6) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of 
Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Sushil Kumar was directed to submit representation in writing to the Commission 
explaining the reasons for not lodging the account and also to lodge account of election expenses with the District 
Election Officer, Jind District within 20 days from the date of receipt of the notice; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind vide his letter no. Elec-2026/1387 dated 17.09.2026 has 
informed that the notice was duly served and received by Sh. Sushil Kumar 's son, on 10.05.2025; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind in his Supplementary Report, vide its letter no. Elec- 
2025/757 dated 10.06.2025 has reported that Sh. Sushil Kumar has not submitted account of election expenses. 
Further, after receipt of the said notice, Sh. Sushil Kumar has neither furnished any reason nor explanation to the 
Election Commission of India, for failure to lodge the account of his election expenses as prescribed under law; and 


WHEREAS, Section 10 A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951 provides that:- 
“If the Election Commission is satisfied that a person- 


(a) has failed to lodge an account of election expenses, within the time and in the manner required by or 
under this Act, and 


(b) has no good reason or justification for the failure, the Election Commission shall, by order published 
in the Official Gazette, declare him to be disqualified and any such person shall be disqualified for a period of three 
years from the date of the order.”’; 


318 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : OCTOBER 31, 2026 / KARTIKA 9, 1948 [PART II—SEC. 3(iii)] 


WHEREAS, on the basis of facts and available records, the Commission is satisfied that Sh. Sushil Kumar 
has failed to lodge account of his election expenses and has no good reason or justification for the failure to do so; and 


NOW, THEREFORE, in pursuance of Section 10A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, the 
Election Commission of India hereby declares Sh. Sushil Kumar, H. No. 679, Village Badhawar, Tehsil Barwala, 
Distt. Hisar, Haryana, a contesting candidate for 35-Safidon Assembly Constituency of Haryana in General 
Election to Legislative Assembly, 2024 to be disqualified for being chosen as and for being a member of either House 
of the Parliament or the Legislative Assembly or the Legislative Council of a State or Union Territory for a period of 
three years from the date of this order.

आ.अ. 125.--यतः, भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा दिनांक 5 सितम्बर, 2024 की अधिसूचना सं0 
464/भा0नि0आ0/हरि0-वि0स0/2024 के अनुसरण में हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधान सभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र के 
लिए साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 आयोजित किया गया था; और 


यतः, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 78 के अनुसार, किसी निर्वाचन में, निर्वाचन लड़ने वाला 
प्रत्येक अभ्यर्थी, निर्वाचित अभ्यर्थी के निर्वाचन की तारीख से 30 दिनों के भीतर अपने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा जिला 
निर्वाचन अधिकारी के पास दाखिल करेगा जो उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 77 के अंतर्गत उसके द्वारा या उसके निर्वाचन 
अभिकर्ता (एजेंट)द्वारा रखे गए लेखे की एक सत्य प्रति होगी; और 


यतः, हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधान सभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र के साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 का 
परिणाम रिटर्निंग आफिसर द्वारा दिनांक 8 अक्तूबर, 2024 को घोषित किया गया था और इस प्रकार निर्वाचन व्यय के 
लेखे दाखिल करने की अन्तिम तारीख 7 नवम्बर, 2024 थी; और 


यतः, निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (1) के अंतर्गत, जिला निर्वाचन 
अधिकारी, जीन्द की रिपोर्ट दिनांक 08.11.2024 जो मुख्य निर्वाचन अधिकारी, हरियाणा से दिनांक 05.02.2025 पत्र 
सं0 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 को प्राप्त हुई थी, के अनुसार श्री विनोद पाल सिंह 
दुलगंच जो हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधानसभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र, 2024 से निर्वाचन लड़ने वाले अभ्यर्थी हैं, 
विधि के अंतर्गत यथापेक्षित रीति से अपने निर्वाचन व्ययों का लेखा दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द की उक्त रिपोर्ट के आधार पर भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा निर्वाचनों का 
संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (5) के अंतर्गत श्री विनोद पाल सिंह दुलगंच को अपने निर्वाचन व्यय के 
लेखे प्रस्तुत नहीं करने के लिए कारण बताओ नोटिस सं.76/हरि/वि.स./व्यय लेखा/2024/सी.ई.एम.एस.-1 दिनांकः 30 
अप्रैल, 2025 को जारी किया गया था, और 


यतः, उपर्युक्त कारण बताओ नोटिस के जरिए और निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप- 
नियम (6) के अनुसार श्री विनोद पाल सिंह दुलगंच को लेखा प्रस्तुत न किए जाने संबंधी कारणों को स्पष्ट करते हुए, 
आयोग को अपना अभ्यावेदन लिखित रूप में प्रस्तुत करने और नोटिस के प्राप्त होने की तारीख से 20 दिनो के भीतर जिला 
निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द को अपने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा प्रस्तुत करने का निदेश दिया गया था, और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द से दिनांक 17.09.2026 के पत्र सं0- निर्वाचन-2026/1397 के द्वारा 
सूचित किया है कि उक्त कारण बताओ नोटिस विधिवत रूप से श्री विनोद पाल सिंह दुलगंच के भतीजे को दिनांक 
09.05.2025 को तामील किया गया था, और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द ने पत्र सं0- निर्वाचन-2025/757 दिनांक 10.06.2025 के द्वारा प्रस्तुत 
की गई अनुपूरक रिपोर्ट में यह बताया है कि श्री विनोद पाल सिंह दुलगंच ने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा प्रस्तुत नहीं किया है। 
इसके अतिरिक्त, उक्त नोटिस की पावती के उपरांत, श्री विनोद पाल सिंह दुलगंच ने विधि के अंतर्गत यथा विहित लेखा 
दाखिल करने में अपनी असफलता के लिए भारत निर्वाचन आयोग को न तो कोई कारण बताया है और न ही कोई 
स्पष्टीकरण दिया है, और 


यतः, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 10क में यह उपबंधित किया गया है किः 


[भाग 1---खण्ड 3(iii)] भारत का राजपत्र : अक्तूबर 31, 2026/ कार्तिक 9, 1948 319 


“यदि Parry आयोग का समाधान at जाता है कि कोई व्यक्ति 


(4) निवच्तिन व्ययों का लेखा उस समय के भीतर और रीति में जैसी इस अधिनियम के द्वारा या अधीन अपेक्षित 
है; दाखिल करने में असफल रहा है, और 


(खि) उस असफलता के लिए कोई अच्छा कारण अथवा aaa नहीं रखता है; तो निर्वाचन आयोग; 
शायकीय राजपत्र में प्रकाशित आदेश द्वारा उसको fae घोषित करेगा और ऐसा व्यक्ति उस आदेश की 
तारीख से तीन वर्ष की कालावधि के लिए fated होगा। 


यतः, तथ्यों और उपलब्ध रिकार्डों के आधार पर आयोग का यह समाधान हो गया है कि श्री विनोद पाल सिंह 
दुलगंच अपने निर्वाचन व्यय के लेखे दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं और उनके पास ऐसा करने में असफल रहने के लिए 
कोई भी उचित कारण अथवा औचित्य नहीं है, और 


अतः, अब, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 10क के अनुसरण में भारत निर्वाचन आयोग एतद्वारा 
घोषणा करता है कि विधान सभा के साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 में हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधान सभा 
निर्वाचन क्षेत्र से निर्वाचन लड़नेवाले अभ्यर्थी श्री विनोद पाल सिंह दुलगंच, बाल्मीकि मन्दिर, नजदीक छोटा बस अड्डा 
अलेवा, तहसील अलेवा, जिला जीन्द, हरियाणा, इस आदेश की तारीख से तीन वर्ष की अवधि के लिए संसद के किसी भी 
सदन अथवा राज्य अथवा संघ राज्य क्षेत्र की विधानसभा अथवा विधान परिषद का सदस्य चुने जाने अथवा होने के लिए

राकेश कुमार, सचिव 


ORDER 
New Delhi, the 22th October, 2026 


O.N. 125.—WHEREAS, the General Election to Legislative Assembly of Haryana, 2024 for 37-Uchana 
Kalan Assembly Constituency was held in pursuance of the Election Commission of India Notification 
No.464/ECI/HAR-LA/2024, dated 50 September, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per Section 78 of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, every contesting candidate at an 
election shall, within 30 days from the date of election of the returned candidate, lodge with the District Election 
Officer an account of his/her election expenses which shall be true copy of the account kept by him/her or his/her 
election agent under Section 77 of the said act; and 


WHEREAS, the result of the above-mentioned election for 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency of 
Haryana, 2024 was declared by the Returning Officer on 8" October, 2024 and hence the last date for lodging the 
account of Election Expenses was 77 November, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per the report submitted by DEO, Jind report dated 08.11.2024 forwarded by the Chief 
Electoral Officer, Haryana vide his letter no. 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 dated 05.02.2025, 
under sub-rule (1)of Rule 89 of the Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Vinod Pal Singh Dulganch a contesting 
candidate from 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency of state of Haryana has failed to lodge account of election 
expenses, as required under law; and 


WHEREAS, on the basis of the said report of the District Election Officer, Jind a Show-Cause notice No. 
76/HR-LA/Exp. Account/2024/CEMS-I, dated 30™ April, 2025 was issued under sub rule (5) of Rule 89 of the 
Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961 by the Election Commission of India to Sh. Vinod Pal Singh Dulganch for not 
lodging of his account of Election Expenses; and 


WHEREAS, through the above said Show Cause Notice and under sub rule (6) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of 
Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Vinod Pal Singh Dulganch was directed to submit representation in writing to the 
Commission explaining the reasons for not lodging the account and also to lodge account of election expenses with 
the District Election Officer, Jind District within 20 days from the date of receipt of the notice; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind vide his letter no. Elec-2026/1387 dated 17.09.2026 has 
informed that the notice was duly served and received by Sh. Vinod Pal Singh Dulganch’s nephew on 09.05.2025; 
and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind in his Supplementary Report, vide its letter no. Elec- 
2025/757 dated 10.06.2025 has reported that Sh. Vinod Pal Singh Dulganch has not submitted account of election 
expenses. Further, after receipt of the said notice, Sh. Vinod Pal Singh Dulganch has neither furnished any reason 


320 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : OCTOBER 31, 2026 / KARTIKA 9, 1948 [PART II—SEC. 3(iii)] 


nor explanation to the Election Commission of India, for failure to lodge the account of his election expenses as 
prescribed under law; and 


WHEREAS, Section 10 A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951 provides that:- 
“If the Election Commission is satisfied that a person- 


(a) has failed to lodge an account of election expenses, within the time and in the manner required by or 
under this Act, and 


(b) has no good reason or justification for the failure, the Election Commission shall, by order published 
in the Official Gazette, declare him to be disqualified and any such person shall be disqualified for a 
period of three years from the date of the order.”’; 


WHEREAS, on the basis of facts and available records, the Commission is satisfied that Sh. Vinod Pal 
Singh Dulganch has failed to lodge account of his election expenses and has no good reason or justification for the 
failure to do so; and 


NOW, THEREFORE, in pursuance of Section 10A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, the 
Election Commission of India hereby declares Sh. Vinod Pal Singh Dulganch, Balmiki Mandir Near Mini Bus 
Stand Alewa, Tehsil Alewa, District Jind, Haryana, a contesting candidate for 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly 
Constituency of Haryana in General Election to Legislative Assembly, 2024 to be disqualified for being chosen as 
and for being a member of either House of the Parliament or the Legislative Assembly or the Legislative Council of a 
State or Union Territory for a period of three years from the date of this order. 


[F. No. 76/HR-LA/37/2024/CEMS-I] 
RAKESH KUMAR, Secy. 


आदेश 
नई दिल्‍ली, 22 अक्तूबर, 2026 


आ.अ. 126.--यतः, भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा दिनांक 5 सितम्बर, 2024 की अधिसूचना 
सं0 464/भा0नि0आ0/हरि0-वि0स0/2024 के अनुसरण में हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधान सभा निर्वाचन 
क्षेत्र के लिए साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 आयोजित किया गया था; और 


यतः, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 78 के अनुसार, किसी निर्वाचन में, निर्वाचन लड़ने वाला 
प्रत्येक अभ्यर्थी, निर्वाचित अभ्यर्थी के निर्वाचन की तारीख से 30 दिनों के भीतर अपने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा जिला 
निर्वाचन अधिकारी के पास दाखिल करेगा जो उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 77 के अंतर्गत उसके द्वारा या उसके निर्वाचन 
अभिकर्ता (एजेंट)द्वारा रखे गए लेखे की एक सत्य प्रति होगी; और 


यतः, हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधान सभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र के साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 का 
परिणाम रिटर्निंग आफिसर द्वारा दिनांक 8 अक्तूबर, 2024 को घोषित किया गया था और इस प्रकार निर्वाचन व्यय के 
लेखे दाखिल करने की अन्तिम तारीख 7 नवम्बर, 2024 थी; और 


यतः, निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (1) के अंतर्गत, जिला निर्वाचन 
अधिकारी, जीन्द की रिपोर्ट दिनांक 08.11.2024 जो मुख्य निर्वाचन अधिकारी, हरियाणा से दिनांक 05.02.2025 पत्र 
सं0 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 को प्राप्त हुई थी, के अनुसार श्री अमरजीत सिंह जो 
हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधानसभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र, 2024 से निर्वाचन लड़ने वाले अभ्यर्थी हैं, विधि के 
अंतर्गत यथापेक्षित रीति से अपने निर्वाचन व्ययों का लेखा दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द की उक्त रिपोर्ट के आधार पर भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा निर्वाचनों का 
संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (5) के अंतर्गत श्री अमरजीत सिंह को अपने निर्वाचन व्यय के लेखे 
प्रस्तुत नहीं करने के लिए कारण बताओ नोटिस सं. 76/हरि/वि.स./व्यय लेखा/2024/सी.ई.एम.एस.-1 दिनांकः 30 अप्रैल, 
2025 को जारी किया गया था, और 


यतः, उपर्युक्त कारण बताओ नोटिस के जरिए और निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप- 
नियम (6) के अनुसार श्री अमरजीत सिंह को लेखा प्रस्तुत न किए जाने संबंधी कारणों को स्पष्ट करते हुए, आयोग को 
अपना अभ्यावेदन लिखित रूप में प्रस्तुत करने और नोटिस के प्राप्त होने की तारीख से 20 दिनो के भीतर जिला निर्वाचन

की गई अनुपूरक रिपोर्ट में यह बताया है कि श्री अमरजीत सिंह ने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा प्रस्तुत नहीं किया है। इसके

विधानसभा अथवा विधान परिषद का सदस्य चुने जाने अथवा होने के लिए निरघिंत होंगे।

राकेश कुमार, सचिव 


ORDER 
New Delhi, the 22th October, 2026 


O.N. 126—WHEREAS, the General Election to Legislative Assembly of Haryana, 2024 for 
37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency was held in pursuance of the Election Commission of India Notification 
No.464/ECI/HAR-LA/2024, dated 50 September, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per Section 78 of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, every contesting candidate at 
an election shall, within 30 days from the date of election of the returned candidate, lodge with the District Election 
Officer an account of his/her election expenses which shall be true copy of the account kept by him/her or his/her 
election agent under Section 77 of the said act; and 


WHEREAS, the result of the above-mentioned election for 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency of 
Haryana, 2024 was declared by the Returning Officer on 8" October, 2024 and hence the last date for lodging the 
account of Election Expenses was 77 November, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per the report submitted by DEO, Jind report dated 08.11.2024 forwarded by the Chief 
Electoral Officer, Haryana vide his letter no. 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 dated 05.02.2025, 
under sub-rule (1) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Amarjeet Singh a contesting candidate 
from 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency of state of Haryana has failed to lodge account of election expenses, 
as required under law; and 


WHEREAS, on the basis of the said report of the District Election Officer, Jind a Show-Cause notice No. 
76/HR-LA/Exp. Account/2024/CEMS-I, dated30™ April, 2025 was issued under sub rule (5) of Rule 89 of the 
Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961 by the Election Commission of India to Sh. Amarjeet Singh for not lodging of his 
account of Election Expenses; and 


322 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : OCTOBER 31, 2026 / KARTIKA 9, 1948 [PART II—SEC. 3(iii)] 


WHEREAS, through the above said Show Cause Notice and under sub rule (6) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of 
Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Amarjeet Singh was directed to submit representation in writing to the Commission 
explaining the reasons for not lodging the account and also to lodge account of election expenses with the District 
Election Officer, Jind District within 20 days from the date of receipt of the notice; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind vide his letter no. Elec-2026/1387 dated 17.09.2026 has 
informed that the notice was duly served and received by Sh. Amarjeet Singh on 09.05.2025; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind in his Supplementary Report, vide its letter no. Elec- 
2025/757 dated 10.06.2025 has reported that Sh. Amarjeet Singh has not submitted account of election expenses. 
Further, after receipt of the said notice, Sh. Amarjeet Singh has neither furnished any reason nor explanation to the 
Election Commission of India, for failure to lodge the account of his election expenses as prescribed under law; and 


WHEREAS, Section 10 A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951 provides that:- 
“If the Election Commission is satisfied that a person- 


(a) has failed to lodge an account of election expenses, within the time and in the manner required by or 
under this Act, and 


(b) has no good reason or justification for the failure, the Election Commission shall, by order published 
in the Official Gazette, declare him to be disqualified and any such person shall be disqualified for a period of three 
years from the date of the order.”’; 


WHEREAS, on the basis of facts and available records, the Commission is satisfied that Sh. Amarjeet 
Singh has failed to lodge account of his election expenses and has no good reason or justification for the failure to do 
so; and 


NOW, THEREFORE, in pursuance of Section 10A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, the 
Election Commission of India hereby declares Sh. Amarjeet Singh, VPO Durana, Tehsil Alewa, District Jind, 
Haryana, a contesting candidate for 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency of Haryana in General Election to 
Legislative Assembly, 2024 to be disqualified for being chosen as and for being a member of either House of the 
Parliament or the Legislative Assembly or the Legislative Council of a State or Union Territory for a period of three 
years from the date of this order. 


[F. No. 76/HR-LA/37/2024/CEMS-I] 
RAKESH KUMAR, Secy.

नई दिल्‍ली, 22 अक्तूबर, 2026 


आ.अ. 127.--यतः, भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा दिनांक 5 सितम्बर, 2024 की अधिसूचना 
HO 464/भा0नि0आ0/हरि0-वि0स0/2024 के अनुसरण में हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना Hat विधान सभा निर्वाचन 
क्षेत्र के लिए साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 आयोजित किया गया था; और 


यतः, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 78 के अनुसार, किसी निर्वाचन में, निर्वाचन लड़ने वाला 
प्रत्येक अभ्यर्थी, निर्वाचित अभ्यर्थी के निर्वाचन की तारीख से 30 दिनों के भीतर अपने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा जिला 
निर्वाचन अधिकारी के पास दाखिल करेगा जो उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 77 के अंतर्गत उसके द्वारा या उसके निर्वाचन 
अभिकर्ता (एजेंट)द्वारा रखे गए लेखे की एक सत्य प्रति होगी; और 


यतः, हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधान सभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र के साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 का 
परिणाम रिटर्निंग आफिसर द्वारा दिनांक 8 अक्तूबर, 2024 को घोषित किया गया था और इस प्रकार निर्वाचन व्यय के 
लेखे दाखिल करने की अन्तिम तारीख 7 नवम्बर, 2024 थी; और 


यतः, निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (1) के अंतर्गत, जिला निर्वाचन 
अधिकारी, जीन्द की रिपोर्ट दिनांक 08.11.2024 जो मुख्य निर्वाचन अधिकारी, हरियाणा से दिनांक 05.02.2025 पत्र 
सं0 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 को प्राप्त हुई थी, के अनुसार श्री गुरमेल सिंह जो 
हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधानसभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र, 2024 से निर्वाचन लड़ने वाले अभ्यर्थी हैं, विधि के 
अंतर्गत यथापेक्षित रीति से अपने निर्वाचन व्ययों का लेखा दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द की उक्त रिपोर्ट के आधार पर भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा निर्वाचनों का 
संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (5) के अंतर्गत श्री गुरमेल सिंह को अपने निर्वाचन व्यय के लेखे प्रस्तुत

नहीं करने के लिए कारण बताओ नोटिस सं. 76/हरि/वि.स./व्यय लेखा/2024/सी.ई.एम.एस.-1 दिनांकः 30 अप्रैल, 2025 
को जारी किया गया था, और 


यतः, उपर्युक्त कारण बताओ नोटिस के जरिए और निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप- 
नियम (6) के अनुसार श्री गुरमेल सिंह को लेखा प्रस्तुत न किए जाने संबंधी कारणों को स्पष्ट करते हुए, आयोग को अपना 
अभ्यावेदन लिखित रूप में प्रस्तुत करने और नोटिस के प्राप्त होने की तारीख से 20 दिनो के भीतर जिला निर्वाचन 
अधिकारी, जीन्द को अपने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा प्रस्तुत करने का निदेश दिया गया था, और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द से दिनांक 17.09.2026 के पत्र सं0- निर्वाचन-2026/1397 के द्वारा 
सूचित किया है कि उक्त कारण बताओ नोटिस विधिवत रूप से श्री गुरमेल सिंह को दिनांक 09.05.2025 को तामील 
किया गया था, और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द ने पत्र सं0- निर्वाचन-2025/757 दिनांक 10.06.2025 के द्वारा प्रस्तुत 
की गई अनुपूरक रिपोर्ट में यह बताया है कि श्री गुरमेल सिंह ने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा प्रस्तुत नहीं किया है। इसके 
अतिरिक्त, उक्त नोटिस की पावती के उपरांत, श्री गुरमेल सिंह ने विधि के अंतर्गत यथा विहित लेखा दाखिल करने में अपनी 
असफलता के लिए भारत निर्वाचन आयोग को न तो कोई कारण बताया है और न ही कोई स्पष्टीकरण दिया है, और 


यतः, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 10क में यह उपबंधित किया गया है किः 
“यदि Parry आयोग का समाधान at जाता है कि कोई व्यक्ति 


(4) निवतच्तिन व्ययों का लेखा उस समय के भीतर और रीति में जैसी इस अधिनियम के द्वारा या अधीन अपेक्षित 
& दाखिल करने में असफल रहा है, और 


(खि) उस असफलता के लिए कोई अच्छा कारण अथवा न्यायोतित्य नहीं रखता है; तो निर्वाचन आयोग; 
शायकीय राजपत्र में प्रकाशित आदेश द्वारा उसको fae घोषित करेगा और ऐसा व्यक्ति उस आदेश की 
तारीख से तीन वर्ष की कालावधि के लिए fated होगा। 


यतः, तथ्यों और उपलब्ध रिकार्डों के आधार पर आयोग का यह समाधान हो गया है कि श्री गुरमेल सिंह अपने 
निर्वाचन व्यय के लेखे दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं और उनके पास ऐसा करने में असफल रहने के लिए कोई भी उचित 
कारण अथवा औचित्य नहीं है, और 


अतः, अब, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 10क के अनुसरण में भारत निर्वाचन आयोग एतद्वारा 
घोषणा करता है कि विधान सभा के साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 में हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधान सभा 
निर्वाचन क्षेत्र से निर्वाचन लड़नेवाले अभ्यर्थी श्री गुरमेल सिंह, गांव शाहपुर, तहसील अलेवा, जिला जीन्द, हरियाणा, इस 
आदेश की तारीख से तीन वर्ष की अवधि के लिए संसद के किसी भी सदन अथवा राज्य अथवा संघ राज्य क्षेत्र की 
विधानसभा अथवा विधान परिषद का सदस्य चुने जाने अथवा होने के लिए निरमघिंत होंगे।

राकेश कुमार, सचिव 


ORDER 
New Delhi, the 22th October, 2026 


O.N. 127.—WHEREAS, the General Election to Legislative Assembly of Haryana, 2024 for 
37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency was held in pursuance of the Election Commission of India Notification 
No.464/ECI/HAR-LA/2024, dated 50 September, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per Section 78 of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, every contesting candidate at 
an election shall, within 30 days from the date of election of the returned candidate, lodge with the District Election 
Officer an account of his/her election expenses which shall be true copy of the account kept by him/her or his/her 
election agent under Section 77 of the said act; and 


WHEREAS, the result of the above-mentioned election for 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency of 
Haryana, 2024 was declared by the Returning Officer on 80 October, 2024 and hence the last date for lodging the 
account of Election Expenses was 77 November, 2024; and 


324 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : OCTOBER 31, 2026 / KARTIKA 9, 1948 [PART II—SEC. 3(iii)] 


WHEREAS, as per the report submitted by DEO, Jind report dated 08.11.2024 forwarded by the Chief 
Electoral Officer, Haryana vide his letter no. 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 dated 05.02.2025, 
under sub-rule (1) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Gurmail Singh a contesting candidate 
from 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency of state of Haryana has failed to lodge account of election expenses, 
as required under law; and 


WHEREAS, on the basis of the said report of the District Election Officer, Jind a Show-Cause notice No. 
76/HR-LA/Exp. Account/2024/CEMS-I, dated 30™ April, 2025 was issued under sub rule (5) of Rule 89 of the 
Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961 by the Election Commission of India to Sh. Gurmail Singh for not lodging of his 
account of Election Expenses; and 


WHEREAS, through the above said Show Cause Notice and under sub rule (6) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of 
Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Gurmail Singh was directed to submit representation in writing to the Commission 
explaining the reasons for not lodging the account and also to lodge account of election expenses with the District 
Election Officer, Jind District within 20 days from the date of receipt of the notice; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind vide his letter no. Elec-2026/1387 dated 17.09.2026 has 
informed that the notice was duly served and received by Sh. Gurmail Singh on 09.05.2025; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind in his Supplementary Report, vide its letter no. Elec- 
2025/757 dated 10.06.2025 has reported that Sh. Gurmail Singh has not submitted account of election expenses. 
Further, after receipt of the said notice, Sh. Gurmail Singh has neither furnished any reason nor explanation to the 
Election Commission of India, for failure to lodge the account of his election expenses as prescribed under law; and 


WHEREAS, Section 10 A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951 provides that:- 
“If the Election Commission is satisfied that a person- 


(a) has failed to lodge an account of election expenses, within the time and in the manner required by or 
under this Act, and 


(b) has no good reason or justification for the failure, the Election Commission shall, by order published 
in the Official Gazette, declare him to be disqualified and any such person shall be disqualified for a 
period of three years from the date of the order.”’; 


WHEREAS, on the basis of facts and available records, the Commission is satisfied that Sh. Gurmail 
Singh has failed to lodge account of his election expenses and has no good reason or justification for the failure to do 
so; and 


NOW, THEREFORE, in pursuance of Section 10A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, the 
Election Commission of India hereby declares Sh. Gurmail Singh, Village Shahpur, Tehsil Alewa, District Jind, 
Haryana, a contesting candidate for 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency of Haryana in General Election to 
Legislative Assembly, 2024 to be disqualified for being chosen as and for being a member of either House of the 
Parliament or the Legislative Assembly or the Legislative Council of a State or Union Territory for a period of three 
years from the date of this order.

आ.अ. 128.--यतः, भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा दिनांक 5 सितम्बर, 2024 की अधिसूचना 
सं0 लिए साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 आयोजित किया गया था; और 


यतः, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 78 के अनुसार, किसी निर्वाचन में, निर्वाचन लड़ने वाला 
प्रत्येक अभ्यर्थी, निर्वाचित अभ्यर्थी के निर्वाचन की तारीख से 30 दिनों के भीतर अपने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा जिला 
निर्वाचन अधिकारी के पास दाखिल करेगा जो उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 77 के अंतर्गत उसके द्वारा या उसके निर्वाचन 
अभिकर्ता (एजेंट)द्वारा रखे गए लेखे की एक सत्य प्रति होगी; और 


[भाग 1---खण्ड 3(iii)] भारत का राजपत्र : अक्तूबर 31, 2026/ कार्तिक 9, 1948 325 


यतः, हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधान सभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र के साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 का 
परिणाम रिटर्निंग आफिसर द्वारा दिनांक 8 अक्तूबर, 2024 को घोषित किया गया था और इस प्रकार निर्वाचन व्यय के 
लेखे दाखिल करने की अन्तिम तारीख 7 नवम्बर, 2024 थी; और 


यतः, निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (1) के अंतर्गत, जिला निर्वाचन 
अधिकारी, जीन्द की रिपोर्ट दिनांक 08.11.2024 जो मुख्य निर्वाचन अधिकारी, हरियाणा से दिनांक 05.02.2025 पत्र 
सं0 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 को प्राप्त हुई थी, के अनुसार श्री देवेन्द्र श्योराण जो 
हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधानसभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र, 2024 से निर्वाचन लड़ने वाले अभ्यर्थी हैं, विधि के 
अंतर्गत यथापेक्षित रीति से अपने निर्वाचन व्ययों का लेखा दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द की उक्त रिपोर्ट के आधार पर भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा निर्वाचनों का 
संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (5) के अंतर्गत श्री देवेन्द्र श्योराण को अपने निर्वाचन व्यय के लेखे 
प्रस्तुत नहीं करने के लिए कारण बताओ नोटिस सं. 76/हरि/वि.स./व्यय लेखा/2024/सी.ई.एम.एस.-1 दिनांकः 30 अप्रैल, 
2025 को जारी किया गया था, और 


यतः, उपर्युक्त कारण बताओ नोटिस के जरिए और निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप- 
नियम (6) के अनुसार श्री देवेन्द्र श्योराण को लेखा प्रस्तुत न किए जाने संबंधी कारणों को स्पष्ट करते हुए, आयोग को 
अपना अभ्यावेदन लिखित रूप में प्रस्तुत करने और नोटिस के प्राप्त होने की तारीख से 20 दिनो के भीतर जिला निर्वाचन 
अधिकारी, जीन्द को अपने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा प्रस्तुत करने का निदेश दिया गया था, और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द से दिनांक 17.09.2026 के पत्र सं0- निर्वाचन-2026/1397 के द्वारा 
सूचित किया है कि उक्त कारण बताओ नोटिस विधिवत रूप से श्री देवेन्द्र श्योराण को दिनांक 12.05.2025 को तामील 
किया गया था, और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द ने पत्र सं0- निर्वाचन-2025/757 दिनांक 10.06.2025 के द्वारा प्रस्तुत 
की गई अनुपूरक रिपोर्ट में यह बताया है कि श्री देवेन्द्र श्योराण ने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा प्रस्तुत नहीं किया है। इसके 
अतिरिक्त, उक्त नोटिस की पावती के उपरांत, श्री देवेन्द्र श्योराण ने विधि के अंतर्गत यथा विहित लेखा दाखिल करने में 
अपनी असफलता के लिए भारत निर्वाचन आयोग को न तो कोई कारण बताया है और न ही कोई स्पष्टीकरण दिया है, और 


यतः, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 10क में यह उपबंधित किया गया है किः 
“यदि निवाचिन आयोग का समाधान at जाता है कि कोई व्यक्ति 


कि... निवचिन व्ययों का लेखा उस समय के भीतर और रीति में जैसी इस अधिनियम के द्वारा या अधीन 
अपेक्षित है; दाखिल करने में असफल रहा है; और 


(खि).. उस असफलता के लिए कोई अच्छा कारण अथवा ATTA नहीं रखता है; तो निर्वाचन आयोग, 
शायकीय राजपत्र में प्रकाशित आदेश द्वारा उसको freed घोषित करेगा और ऐसा व्यक्ति TT 
आदेश की तारीख से तीन वर्ष की TATA के लिए ।निराहित होया। 


यतः, तथ्यों और उपलब्ध रिकार्डों के आधार पर आयोग का यह समाधान हो गया है किश्री देवेन्द्र श्योराण अपने 
निर्वाचन व्यय के लेखे दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं और उनके पास ऐसा करने में असफल रहने के लिए कोई भी उचित 
कारण अथवा औचित्य नहीं है, और 


अतः, अब, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 10क के अनुसरण में भारत निर्वाचन आयोग एतद्वारा 
घोषणा करता है कि विधान सभा के साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 में हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधान सभा 
निर्वाचन क्षेत्र से निर्वाचन लड़नेवाले अभ्यर्थी श्री देवेन्द्र श्योराण, गांव नाकीपुर, तहसील लोहारू, जिला भिवानी, 
हरियाणा, इस आदेश की तारीख से तीन वर्ष की अवधि के लिए संसद के किसी भी सदन अथवा राज्य अथवा संघ राज्य

326 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : OCTOBER 31, 2026 / KARTIKA 9, 1948 [PART II—SEC. 3(iii)] 


ORDER 
New Delhi, the 22th October, 2026 


O.N. 128.— WHEREAS, the General Election to Legislative Assembly of Haryana, 2024 for 37-Uchana 
Kalan Assembly Constituency was held in pursuance of the Election Commission of India Notification 
No.464/ECI/HAR-LA/2024, dated 5" September, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per Section 78 of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, every contesting candidate at 
an election shall, within 30 days from the date of election of the returned candidate, lodge with the District Election 
Officer an account of his/her election expenses which shall be true copy of the account kept by him/her or his/her 
election agent under Section 77 of the said act; and 


WHEREAS, the result of the above-mentioned election for 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency of 
Haryana, 2024 was declared by the Returning Officer on 80 October, 2024 and hence the last date for lodging the 
account of Election Expenses was 77 November, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per the report submitted by DEO, Jind report dated 08.11.2024 forwarded by the Chief 
Electoral Officer, Haryana vide his letter no. 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692dated 05.02.2025, 
under sub-rule (1) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Devender Sheoran a contesting candidate 
from 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency of state of Haryana has failed to lodge account of election expenses, 
as required under law; and 


WHEREAS, on the basis of the said report of the District Election Officer, Jind a Show-Cause notice No. 
76/HR-LA/Exp. Account/2024/CEMS-I, dated 307 April, 2025 was issued under sub rule (5) of Rule 89 of the 
Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961 by the Election Commission of India to Sh. Devender Sheoran for not lodging of 
his account of Election Expenses; and 


WHEREAS, through the above said Show Cause Notice and under sub rule (6) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of 
Elections Rules, 1961, Sh. Devender Sheoran was directed to submit representation in writing to the Commission 
explaining the reasons for not lodging the account and also to lodge account of election expenses with the District 
Election Officer, Jind District within 20 days from the date of receipt of the notice; and 


WHEREAS, theDistrict Election Officer, Jind vide his letter no. Elec-2026/1387 dated 17.09.2026 has 
informed that the notice was duly served and received by Sh. Devender Sheoran on 12.05.2025; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind in his Supplementary Report, vide its letter no. Elec- 
2025/757 dated 10.06.2025 has reported that Sh. Devender Sheoran has not submitted account of election expenses. 
Further, after receipt of the said notice, Sh. Devender Sheoran has neither furnished any reason nor explanation to 
the Election Commission of India, for failure to lodge the account of his election expenses as prescribed under law; 
and 


WHEREAS, Section 10 A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951 provides that:- 
“If the Election Commission is satisfied that a person- 


(a) has failed to lodge an account of election expenses, within the time and in the manner required by or 
under this Act, and 


(b) has no good reason or justification for the failure, the Election Commission shall, by order published 
in the Official Gazette, declare him to be disqualified and any such person shall be disqualified for a 
period of three years from the date of the order.”’; 


WHEREAS, on the basis of facts and available records, the Commission is satisfied that Sh. Devender 
Sheoran has failed to lodge account of his election expenses and has no good reason or justification for the failure to 
do so; and 


NOW, THEREFORE, in pursuance of Section 10A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, the 
Election Commission of India hereby declares Sh. Devender Sheoran, VPO Nakipur, Tehsil Loharu, District 
Bhiwani, Haryana,a contesting candidate for 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency of Haryana in General 
Election to Legislative Assembly, 2024 to be disqualified for being chosen as and for being a member of either House 
of the Parliament or the Legislative Assembly or the Legislative Council of a State or Union Territory for a period of 
three years from the date of this order. 


[F. No. 76/HR-LA/37/2024/CEMS-I] 
RAKESH KUMAR, Secy.

आ.अ. 129.--- यतः, भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा दिनांक 5 सितम्बर, 2024 की अधिसूचना 
HO 64/भा0नि0आ0/हरि0-वि0स0/2024 के अनुसरण में हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधान सभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र 
के लिए साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 आयोजित किया गया था; और 


यतः, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 78 के अनुसार, किसी निर्वाचन में, निर्वाचन लड़ने 
वाला प्रत्येक अभ्यर्थी, निर्वाचित अभ्यर्थी के निर्वाचन की तारीख से 30 दिनों के भीतर अपने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा जिला 
निर्वाचन अधिकारी के पास दाखिल करेगा जो उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 77 के अंतर्गत उसके द्वारा या उसके निर्वाचन 
अभिकर्ता (एजेंट)द्वारा रखे गए लेखे की एक सत्य प्रति होगी; और 


यतः, हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधान सभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र के साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 का 
परिणाम रिटर्निंग आफिसर द्वारा दिनांक 8 अक्तूबर, 2024 को घोषित किया गया था और इस प्रकार निर्वाचन व्यय के 
लेखे दाखिल करने की अन्तिम तारीख 7 नवम्बर, 2024 थी; और 


यतः, निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (1) के अंतर्गत, जिला निर्वाचन 
अधिकारी, जीन्द की रिपोर्ट दिनांक 08.11.2024 जो मुख्य निर्वाचन अधिकारी, हरियाणा से दिनांक 05.02.2025 पत्र 
सं0 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 को प्राप्त हुई थी, के अनुसार कॉमरेड रमेश खटकड़ जो 
हरियाणा राज्य के 37-उचाना कलां विधानसभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र, 2024 से निर्वाचन लड़ने वाले अभ्यर्थी हैं, विधि के 
अंतर्गत यथापेक्षित रीति से अपने निर्वाचन व्ययों का लेखा दाखिल करने में असफल रहे हैं और

30 अप्रैल, 2025 को जारी किया गया था, और

ORDER 
New Delhi, the 22th October, 2026 


O.N. 129.— WHEREAS, the General Election to Legislative Assembly of Haryana, 2024 for 
37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency was held in pursuance of the Election Commission of India Notification 
No.464/ECI/HAR-LA/2024, dated 5" September, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per Section 78 of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, every contesting candidate at 
an election shall, within 30 days from the date of election of the returned candidate, lodge with the District Election 
Officer an account of his/her election expenses which shall be true copy of the account kept by him/her or his/her 
election agent under Section 77 of the said act; and 


WHEREAS, the result of the above-mentioned election for 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency of 
Haryana, 2024 was declared by the Returning Officer on 80 October, 2024 and hence the last date for lodging the 
account of Election Expenses was 77 November, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per the report submitted by DEO, Jind report dated 08.11.2024 forwarded by the Chief 
Electoral Officer, Haryana vide his letter no. 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 dated 05.02.2025, 
under sub-rule (1) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961, Comrade Ramesh Khatkar a contesting 
candidate from 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly Constituency of state of Haryana has failed to lodge account of election 
expenses, as required under law; and 


WHEREAS, on the basis of the said report of the District Election Officer, Jind a Show-Cause notice No. 
76/HR-LA/Exp. Account/2024/CEMS-I, dated 307 April, 2025 was issued under sub rule (5) of Rule 89 of the 
Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961 by the Election Commission of India to Comrade Ramesh Khatkar for not 
lodging of his account of Election Expenses; and 


WHEREAS, through the above said Show Cause Notice and under sub rule (6) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of 
Elections Rules, 1961, Comrade Ramesh Khatkar was directed to submit representation in writing to the 
Commission explaining the reasons for not lodging the account and also to lodge account of election expenses with 
the District Election Officer, Jind District within 20 days from the date of receipt of the notice; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind vide his letter no. Elec-2026/1387 dated 17.09.2026 has 
informed that the notice was duly served and received by Comrade Ramesh Khatkar’s wife (name Smt. Praveen) 
on 10.05.2025; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind in his Supplementary Report, vide its letter no. Elec- 
2025/757 dated 10.06.2025 has reported that Comrade Ramesh Khatkar has not submitted account of election 
expenses. Further, after receipt of the said notice, Comrade Ramesh Khatkar has neither furnished any reason nor 
explanation to the Election Commission of India, for failure to lodge the account of his election expenses as 
prescribed under law; and 


WHEREAS, Section 10 A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951 provides that:- 
“Tf the Election Commission is satisfied that a person- 


(a) as failed to lodge an account of election expenses, within the time and in the manner required by or 
under this Act, and 


(b) as no good reason or justification for the failure, the Election Commission shall, by order published 


[भाग 1---खण्ड 3(iii)] भारत का राजपत्र : अक्तूबर 31, 2026/ कार्तिक 9, 1948 329 


in the Official Gazette, declare him to be disqualified and any such person shall be disqualified for a 
period of three years from the date of the order.”’; 


WHEREAS, on the basis of facts and available records, the Commission is satisfied that Comrade Ramesh 
Khatkar has failed to lodge account of his election expenses and has no good reason or justification for the failure to 
do so; and 


NOW, THEREFORE, in pursuance of Section 10A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, the 
Election Commission of India hereby declares Comrade Ramesh Khatkar, H. No. 652/20, Gali No. 3, Harinagar, 
Narwana Tehsil, Narwana, District Jind, Haryana, a contesting candidate for 37-Uchana Kalan Assembly 
Constituency of Haryana in General Election to Legislative Assembly, 2024 to be disqualified for being chosen as 
and for being a member of either House of the Parliament or the Legislative Assembly or the Legislative Council of a 
State or Union Territory for a period of three years from the date of this order. 


[F. No. 76/HR-LA/37/2024/CEMS-I] 
RAKESH KUMAR, Secy.

आ.अ. 130.-- यतः, भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा दिनांक 5 सितम्बर, 2024 की अधिसूचना 
सं0 464/भा0नि0आ0/हरि0-वि0स0/2024 के अनुसरण में हरियाणा राज्य के 38-नरवाना (अ0जा0) विधान सभा 
निर्वाचन क्षेत्र के लिए साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 आयोजित किया गया था; और 


यतः, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 78 के अनुसार, किसी निर्वाचन में, निर्वाचन लड़ने वाला 
प्रत्येक अभ्यर्थी, निर्वाचित अभ्यर्थी के निर्वाचन की तारीख से 30 दिनों के भीतर अपने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा जिला 
निर्वाचन अधिकारी के पास दाखिल करेगा जो उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 77 के अंतर्गत उसके द्वारा या उसके निर्वाचन 
अभिकर्ता (एजेंट)द्वारा रखे गए लेखे की एक सत्य प्रति होगी; और 


यतः, हरियाणा राज्य के 38-नरवाना (अ0जा0) विधान सभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र के साधारण निर्वाचन, 2024 का 
परिणाम रिटर्निंग आफिसर द्वारा दिनांक 8 अक्तूबर, 2024 को घोषित किया गया था और इस प्रकार निर्वाचन व्यय के 
लेखे दाखिल करने की अन्तिम तारीख 7 नवम्बर, 2024 थी; और 


यतः, निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (1) के अंतर्गत, जिला निर्वाचन 
अधिकारी, जीन्द की रिपोर्ट दिनांक 08.11.2024 जो मुख्य निर्वाचन अधिकारी, हरियाणा से दिनांक 05.02.2025 पत्र 
सं0 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 को प्राप्त हुई थी, के अनुसार सुश्री संतोष दनौदा जो 
हरियाणा राज्य के 38-नरवाना (अ0जा0) विधानसभा निर्वाचन क्षेत्र, 2024 से निर्वाचन लड़ने वाली अभ्यर्थी हैं, विधि के 
अंतर्गत यथापेक्षित रीति से अपनेनिर्वाचन व्ययों का लेखा दाखिल करने में असफल रही हैं और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द की उक्त रिपोर्ट के आधार पर भारत निर्वाचन आयोग द्वारा निर्वाचनों का 
संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप-नियम (5) के अंतर्गत सुश्री संतोष दनौदा को अपने निर्वाचन व्यय के लेखे 
प्रस्तुत नहीं करने के लिए कारण बताओ नोटिस सं.76/हरि/वि.स./व्यय लेखा/2024/सी.ई.एम.एस.-1 दिनांकः 30 अप्रैल, 
2025 को जारी किया गया था, और 


यतः, उपर्युक्त कारण बताओ नोटिस के जरिए और निर्वाचनों का संचालन नियम, 1961 के नियम 89 के उप- 
नियम (6) के अनुसार सुश्री संतोष दनौदा को लेखा प्रस्तुत न किए जाने संबंधी कारणों को स्पष्ट करते हुए, आयोग को 
अपना अभ्यावेदन लिखित रूप में प्रस्तुत करने और नोटिस के प्राप्त होने की तारीख से 20 दिनो के भीतर जिला निर्वाचन 
अधिकारी, जीन्द को अपने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा प्रस्तुत करने का निदेश दिया गया था, और 


यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द से दिनांक 17.09.2026 के पत्र सं0- निर्वाचन-2026/139 के द्वारा 
सूचित किया है कि उक्त कारण बताओ नोटिस विधिवत रूप से सुश्री संतोष दनौदा के भाई (अनिल कुमार) को दिनांक 
10.05.2025 को तामील किया गया था, और

यतः, जिला निर्वाचन अधिकारी, जीन्द ने पत्र सं0- निर्वाचन-2025/757 दिनांक 10.06.2025 के द्वारा प्रस्तुत 
की गई अनुपूरक रिपोर्ट में यह बताया है कि सुश्री संतोष दनौदा ने निर्वाचन व्यय का लेखा प्रस्तुत नहीं किया है। इसके 
अतिरिक्त, उक्त नोटिस की पावती के उपरांत, सुश्री संतोष दनौदा ने विधि के अंतर्गत यथा विहित लेखा दाखिल करने में 
अपनी असफलता के लिए भारत निर्वाचन आयोग को न तो कोई कारण बताया है और न ही कोई स्पष्टीकरण दिया है, और 


यतः, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 10क में यह उपबंधित किया गया है किः 
“यदि Parry आयोग का समाधान at जाता है कि कोई व्यक्ति 


(4) निवच्तिन व्ययों का लेखा उस समय के भीतर और रीति में जैसी इस अधिनियम के द्वारा या अधीन अपेक्षित 
है; दाखिल करने में असफल रहा है, और 


(खि) उस असफलता के लिए कोई अच्छा कारण अथवा aaa नहीं रखता है; तो निर्वाचन आयोग; 
शायकीय राजपत्र में प्रकाशित आदेश द्वारा उसको bed घोषित करेगा और ऐसा व्यक्ति उस आदेश की 
तारीख से तीन वर्ष की कालावधि के लिए faced होगा। 


यतः, तथ्यों और उपलब्ध रिकार्डों के आधार पर आयोग का यह समाधान हो गया है कि सुश्री संतोष दनौदा 
अपने निर्वाचन व्यय के लेखे दाखिल करने में असफल रही हैं और उनके पास ऐसा करने में असफल रहने के लिए कोई भी 
उचित कारण अथवा औचित्य नहीं है, और 


अतः, अब, लोक प्रतिनिधित्व अधिनियम, 1951 की धारा 10क के अनुसरण में भारत निर्वाचन आयोग एतद्वारा

ORDER 
New Delhi, the 22th October, 2026 


O.N. 130.—WHEREAS, the General Election to Legislative Assembly of Haryana, 2024 for 
38-Narwana (SC) Assembly Constituency was held in pursuance of the Election Commission of India Notification 
No.464/ECI/HAR-LA/2024, dated 50 September, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per Section 78 of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, every contesting candidate at 
an election shall, within 30 days from the date of election of the returned candidate, lodge with the District Election 
Officer an account of his/her election expenses which shall be true copy of the account kept by him/her or his/her 
election agent under Section 77 of the said act; and 


WHEREAS, the result of the above-mentioned election for 38-Narwana (SC) Assembly Constituency of 
Haryana, 2024 was declared by the Returning Officer on 8" October, 2024 and hence the last date for lodging the 
account of Election Expenses was 77 November, 2024; and 


WHEREAS, as per the report submitted by DEO, Jind report dated 08.11.2024 forwarded by the Chief 
Electoral Officer, Haryana vide his letter no. 6/Elect-Exp./GELA-HR-2024/Elec-2025/2AE-692 dated 05.02.2025, 
under sub-rule (1)of Rule 89 of the Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961, Ms. Santosh Danodaa contesting candidate 
from 38-Narwana (SC) Assembly Constituency of state of Haryana has failed to lodge account of election expenses, 
as required under law; and 


WHEREAS, on the basis of the said report of the District Election Officer, Jind a Show-Cause notice No. 
76/HR-LA/Exp. Account/2024/CEMS-I, dated 30™ April, 2025 was issued under sub rule (5) of Rule 89 of the 
Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961 by the Election Commission of India to Ms. Santosh Danoda for not lodging of 
her account of Election Expenses; and 


WHEREAS, through the above said Show Cause Notice and under sub rule (6) of Rule 89 of the Conduct of 
Elections Rules, 1961, Ms. Santosh Danoda was directed to submit representation in writing to the Commission 


[भाग 1---खण्ड 3(iii)] भारत का राजपत्र : अक्तूबर 31, 2026/ कार्तिक 9, 1948 331 


explaining the reasons for not lodging the account and also to lodge account of election expenses with the District 
Election Officer, Jind District within 20 days from the date of receipt of the notice; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind vide his letter no. Elec-2026/1387 dated 17.09.2026 has 
informed that the notice was duly served and received by Ms. Santosh Danoda’s brother (name Anil Kumar) on 
10.05.2025; and 


WHEREAS, the District Election Officer, Jind in his Supplementary Report, vide its letter no. Elec- 
2025/757 dated 10.06.2025 has reported that Ms. Santosh Danoda has not submitted account of election expenses. 
Further, after receipt of the said notice, Ms. Santosh Danoda has neither furnished any reason nor explanation to the 
Election Commission of India, for failure to lodge the account of her election expenses as prescribed under law; and 


WHEREAS, Section 10 A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951 provides that:- 
“If the Election Commission is satisfied that a person- 


(a) has failed to lodge an account of election expenses, within the time and in the manner required by 
or under this Act, and 


(b) has no good reason or justification for the failure, the Election Commission shall, by order 
published in the Official Gazette, declare him to be disqualified and any such person shall be 


0, 


disqualified for a period of three years from the date of the order. 7, 


WHEREAS, on the basis of facts and available records, the Commission is satisfied that Ms. Santosh 
Danoda has failed to lodge account of her election expenses and has no good reason or justification for the failure to 
do so; and 


NOW, THEREFORE, in pursuance of Section 10A of the Representation of the People Act, 1951, the 
Election Commission of India hereby declares Ms. Santosh Danoda,Village Danoda Kalan, Teh. Narwana, 
District Jind, Haryana, a contesting candidate for 38-Narwana (SC) Assembly Constituencyof Haryana in General 
Election to Legislative Assembly, 2024 to be disqualified for being chosen as and for being a member of either House 
of the Parliament or the Legislative Assembly or the Legislative Council of a State or Union Territory for a period of 
three years from the date of this order. 


[F. No. 76/HR-LA/38/2024/CEMS-I] 
RAKESH KUMAR, Secy.

Court No. - 89 


HON'BLE RAJ BEER SINGH, J. 
Order on Civil Misc. Application No. 4 of 2024 


1. This Civil Misc. Application under Order VII Rule 11 Code of Civil Procedure has been filed on behalf of 
respondent / returned candidate Dr. Vinod Kumar Bind seeking following prayer: 


"It is therefore most respectfully prayed that the election petition be dismissed under Order VII 
Rule 11 of the Code of Civil Procedure as it discloses no cause of action." 


2. For constitution of 18th Lok Sabha, election schedule was notified by the Election Commission of India on 
16.03.2024 and elections for Lok Sabha were to be conducted in seven phases. In 78- Bhadohi (Uttar Pradesh) 
Parliamentary constituency the nomination for contesting election were to be submitted up-to 06.05.2024 and date of 
scrutiny of nomination papers was 07.05.2024. The petitioner filed his nomination paper on 04.05.2024 to contest 
election for Member of Parliament from 78 — Bhadohi Parliamentary Constituency and after scrutiny his nomination 
papers were found valid. After date of withdrawal of the nomination papers i.e. 09.05.2024, the Returning Officer has 
issued a list of contesting candidates and there were total 10 candidates, whose nomination papers were found valid 
and they were allotted symbols. The poll took place on 25.05.2024, followed by counting of votes on 04.06.2024. 
After counting, the Returning Officer declared that total 10,85,742 votes were polled and as per final result sheet, 
petitioner Lalitesh Pati Tripathi has got 4,15,910 votes and respondent / returned candidate Dr. Vinod Kumar Bind got 
4,59,982 votes and accordingly Dr. Vinod Kumar Bind was declared elected as Member of Parliament from the 
aforesaid constituency. 


3. The petitioner has challenged the election of the returned candidate on the following five grounds :- 


A. Because the result of the election in so far as it concerned to Returned Candidate namely Dr 
Vinod Kumar Bind, is materially affected due to improper acceptance of his nomination paper as a 
Member of Parliament from 78- Bhadohi Parliamentary constituency of District Bhadohi, by the 
Returning Officer, 78-Bhadohi Parliamentary constituency of District Bhadohi. 


B. Because the election of the Returned Candidate Dr Vinod Kumar Bind as a Member of 
Parliament from 78- Bhadohi Parliamentary constituency of District Bhadohi, is illegal and void as 
Returned Candidate was not qualified and was disqualified under the provision of Constitution of India 
to contest the election on the post of Member of Parliament from 78 — Bhadohi Parliamentary 
constituency of District Bhadohi. 


C. Because the election of the Returned Candidate Dr Vinod Kumar Bind as a Member of 
Parliament from 78- Bhadohi Parliamentary constituency of District Bhadohi, is illegal and void due to 
improper rejection of nomination paper of Candidate Zia-Ul-Haq and Lalti Devi. 


12. Because the result of election, in so far as it concern the Respondent, is materially affected by 
non-compliance of the provisions of Constitution of India or Representation of People Act, 1971 or 
Conduct Rules, 1961 or Orders made therein, and the orders issued by the Election Commission of India 
from time to time in exercise of its powers conferred by the Constitution as well as the instructions 
contained in the hand book of Returning officers issued by the E.C.I. in exercising powers under Article 
324 of the Constitution of India. 


E Because the respondent, and his agent or any person with his consent committed the corrupt 
practice of bribery in the meaning of section 123(1)(B)(a) with explanation of the R.P. Act, 1951. 


4. The respondent / returned candidate filed aforesaid application under Order VII Rule 11 CPC, alleging that 
election petition does not disclose cause of action and the election petition is liable to be dismissed under Order VII 
Rule 11 CPC. It was alleged that election petition is completely bereft of material facts and that averments made in 
the election petition do not amount to any ground for setting aside the election of the returned candidate and petition 
does not raise any triable issue. The necessary material facts and ingredients in respect of grounds and allegations 
have not been stated in the election petition. The Election petition contains indefinite, vague, general and 
misconceived allegations, which do not amount to any material facts. There is no such material fact which may 
indicate any material effect upon election of the returned candidate / respondent. The pleadings in the election petition 
are frivolous, vexatious, unnecessary, irrelevant and tend to prejudice, embrace and delay the fair trial of election 
petition. The election petition purports to contain alleged grounds of improper rejection of nomination papers of two 
candidates, namely, Zia-Ul- Haq and Lalti Devi and improper acceptance of nomination paper of the respondent as 
well as of corrupt practice of bribery but there are no material facts to support any of the alleged ground raised in the 
election petition. The petitioner has alleged that respondent was disqualified for being chosen as a Member of 
Parliament under Tenth Schedule of the Constitution as he has not resigned from membership of Nirbal Indian 


334 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : OCTOBER 31, 2026 / KARTIKA 9, 1948 [PART II—SEC. 3(iii)] 


Shoshit Hamara Aam Dal party (hereinafter referred as NISHAD Party) before contesting the parliamentary election 
as a candidate of Bhartiya Janta Party (hereinafter referred as BJP) but said allegation is wholly misconceived 
inasmuch as Tenth schedule of the Constitution of India is not applicable in the present matter. Regarding alleged 
improper rejection of the nomination papers of Zia-Ul- Haq and Smt. Lalti Devi, the petitioner has not disclosed even 
the grounds of rejection of their nomination papers and merely a vague allegation was made that their nominations 
were rejected on flimsy grounds, which goes to show that petitioner does not know as to on what ground nomination

respondent in regard to alleged corrupt practice. It was stated that petition is liable to be dismissed under Order VII 
Rule 11 Civil Procedure Code (hereinafter after referred as CPC). 


5. The petitioner has filed reply / counter affidavit against the application under Order VII Rule 11 CPC, 
alleging that the petitioner has filed present election petition on the grounds, which come under purview of Section 
100 of Representation of the People Act 1951 (hereinafter referred as R.P. Act) and pleadings of election petition are 
complete in all respect and petitioner has supplied all material facts and particulars in respect of the grounds 
mentioned in the election petition. The pleadings of election petition disclose complete cause of action and that 
application under Order VII Rule 11 CPC is misconceived and the same is filed with intention to delay the fair trial. 
None of the averment / allegation made in the election petition is frivolous, vexatious, unnecessary or irrelevant and 
in fact pleadings of election petition are precise, definite and specific. The improper rejection of the nomination 
papers of candidate Zia-Ul- Haq and Smt. Lalti Devi comes under the purview of Section 100(1)(c) of R.P. Act. In 
respect of improper acceptance of nomination paper of the respondent, the petition falls within the purview of Section 
100(1)(d)G) of R.P. Act and similarly corrupt practice of bribery comes under purview of Section 100 (1)(b) of R.P. 
Act read with Section 123(1)(B)(a) of R.P. Act. It was further mentioned that material facts and particulars relating to 
grounds A, B, C, D, E have been provided in para nos. 28 to 105 and para nos. | to 26 of the election petition. The 
returned candidate / respondent contested the parliamentary election as a candidate of BJP while he was a sitting 
member of U.P. Legislative Assembly from NISHAD party from Majhwan Assembly Constituency and thus 
provisions of Tenth Schedule of Constitution are fully attracted and applicable and returned candidate was not 
qualified and in fact disqualified to contest the parliamentary election. The election petition constitutes a complete 
cause of action and none of the paragraph of election petition is irrelevant, frivolous or vexatious, rather in fact 
pleadings of the election petition are definite, precise and disclosed complete cause of action. The application under 
Order VII Rule 11 CPC was filed by the returned candidate /respondent to delay the fair trial and the same is liable to 
be rejected. 


6. Heard Sri K. R. Singh learned counsel for the respondent / returned candidate and Sri N. K. Pandey learned 
counsel for the election petitioner at length and perused the record. 


7. Learned counsel for the respondent / returned candidate submitted that the election petition does not contain 
any material fact to disclose a cause of action. The ground of alleged disqualification of the respondent taken by the 
petitioner to challenge the election of the respondent is misconceived. It was submitted that Article 102 of the 
Constitution of India is in two parts and there is difference between Article 102 (1) and 102 (2). Article 102(1) of the 
Constitution provides about disqualification ‘for being chosen’ and ‘for being’ both, whereas, Article 102 (2) provides 
disqualification for ‘being’ only and not for ‘being chosen". Paragraph 2 of Tenth Schedule provides about 
disqualification 'for being' a Member of the House and it is not a disqualification ‘for being chosen’, meaning thereby 
that under Article 102 (2) read with paragraph 2 of Tenth Schedule, a sitting member of the House shall be 
disqualified 'for being’ or for continuing as a member of that House till the term of that house but it is not a 
disqualification to contest a fresh election. A member of one House can contest fresh election of another House either 
from the same party or from different party. The allegation made by petitioner is totally misconceived and it is not a 
sustainable ground under Section 100 of the R.P. Act to challenge the election of the returned candidate.
